4490 — roll ball trapezius upper

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4490 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: source_defined_support.

Self-massage with a small ball to ease tension in the upper trapezius. A simple way to loosen tight spots before or after training.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Place the ball under your upper trapezius while leaning back against a wall.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 4490.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Preserve any source-defined grip, crossed-arm position or moving-arm cue. Otherwise rest the arms clear of the moving segment in a comfortable balance position for owner review.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 4490.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Preserve the source's documented knee, thigh, hip or foot support points; leave unspecified pad settings and finer leg geometry for owner review rather than inventing dimensions.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 4490.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Massage ball, Wall surface. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 4490.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Place the ball under your upper trapezius while leaning back against a wall. 4490.json#/instructions/0
  2. Shift your weight onto the ball until you feel moderate, tolerable pressure. 4490.json#/instructions/1
  3. Roll slowly back and forth over a short range, about 2-3 cm at a time. 4490.json#/instructions/2
  4. When you find a tender spot, pause on it and breathe for 20-30 seconds. 4490.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your breathing steady and avoid rolling over bone or joints. 4490.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat on the other side if needed. 4490.json#/instructions/5

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4493 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: side_lying.

Self-massage with a small ball to ease tension in the side of the back (latissimus dorsi). A simple way to loosen tight spots before or after training.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Place the ball under your side of the back (latissimus dorsi) while lying on your side with your arm overhead.
